This folder holds one GPU kernel task on an Apple M4 Mac (PyTorch 2.8.0 on the GPU, device "mps").

- kernel.py: kernel(q, k, v), causal attention. It is the only code file you may edit.
- reference.py: the reference answer and the inputs it is timed on (batch 8, 8 heads, 1024 positions, head size 64, float32).
- bench.py: times kernel.py against reference() on fresh inputs and prints the speedup. You may use it 10 times: ~/miniconda3/bin/python3 bench.py
  A run usually returns in under a minute; give the command a timeout of at least 10 minutes. If it is cut off, ~/miniconda3/bin/python3 bench.py --wait gets the result.
- check.py: checks kernel.py's answer without timing. Use it as often as you like: ~/miniconda3/bin/python3 check.py

Rules:
- kernel(q, k, v) keeps its name and arguments and returns a GPU tensor with the reference's shape, within the tolerance in reference.py.
- Anything that runs on this Mac counts, for example a custom Metal shader through torch.mps.compile_shader.
- kernel.py may not read any file.
- Time only with bench.py; other jobs share this GPU.
- Work only inside this folder. Do not read, list or search anything outside it.

Unchanged, kernel.py runs in 67.1 ms (speedup 1.00x).

Make kernel() as fast as you can. LESSONS.md in this folder holds notes from earlier agents who did this same task. Read it before your first bench run. Notes from the previous round of agents, one section per agent.

## Agent 1

The original kernel was reported as 67.6 milliseconds. All ten benchmark runs were used. Reference times varied from 57.91 to 72.17 milliseconds, so compare absolute kernel times as well as speedups.

Built-in causal scaled dot product attention took 17.01 milliseconds in float32 and 10.64 milliseconds with half inputs and a float output. Both passed.

The fastest tested implementation was a custom Metal tiled attention shader using SIMD group matrix operations. Each SIMD group handles eight query rows and all 64 output columns. Stage queries, keys, and values as half precision; accumulate scores and outputs in float32. Keep online softmax maxima and denominators in float32, reduce each row using lane shuffles, and skip wholly future key tiles. This avoids materializing the full attention matrix. Compile the shader once at import.

Query/key tile sizes and times: 32 by 64, 8.04 milliseconds; 64 by 64, 7.04 milliseconds; 64 by 32, 7.53 milliseconds; 128 by 64, 10.61 milliseconds. The final kernel restores 64 by 64, which achieved an 8.23-fold benchmark speedup and relative error 0.00061.

Other slower experiments: sixteen query rows per SIMD group with 64 by 32 tiles, 8.55 milliseconds; vectorized four-element staging, 7.43 milliseconds; preconverting keys and values with PyTorch, 8.80 milliseconds; reusing query shared storage for keys, 7.77 milliseconds.

Half precision output accumulation is unsafe: repeated fresh-input checks produced errors up to 0.00364, exceeding the 0.002 tolerance. Retain float32 accumulation.

## Agent 2

The supplied unchanged baseline was 67.6 milliseconds. All ten timing runs passed correctness. The fastest measured kernel took 4.99 milliseconds, with a simultaneous reference time of 59.93 milliseconds and a reported speedup of 12.01 times. Its benchmark relative error was 0.00061, below the allowed 0.002.

Built-in causal scaled dot product attention took 16.93 milliseconds using float32. Casting its three inputs to half precision and its output back to float32 reduced this to 10.57 milliseconds.

A custom Metal tiled attention shader improved further. Use half precision inputs and probabilities, float32 dot product accumulation, float32 scores, and stable online softmax with float32 running maxima and sums. Skip key tiles beyond the causal boundary. Return float32 output. Eight SIMD groups, totaling 256 threads, cooperate per query tile using eight by eight SIMD matrix operations.

With 32 queries and 32 keys per tile, shared partial outputs took 6.95 milliseconds. Increasing the key tile to 64 took 5.80 milliseconds. Keeping partial output matrices in registers instead took 5.26 milliseconds. Increasing queries to 64 produced the best 4.99 milliseconds.

Rearranging matrix products to share more operand loads was slower, taking 7.41 milliseconds. Half precision scores sharing storage with probabilities did not improve observed times: 64 queries with 128 keys took 5.98 milliseconds; 64 with 64 took 6.06 milliseconds during a reported busy GPU run; 128 with 64 took 6.39 milliseconds.

Reference times varied from 58.65 to 77.12 milliseconds because the GPU is shared. Treat small differences cautiously. The final implementation retains the fastest measured 64 query, 64 key, float32 score variant.

## Agent 3

The original kernel was reported at 67.6 ms. Ten benchmark submissions were used; reference times varied from 58.89 to 72.08 ms, so compare kernel times as well as speedups.

PyTorch scaled dot product attention with causal masking took 16.81 ms in float32. Converting all three inputs to half, using the same operation, and returning float32 took 10.52 ms. Both passed correctness.

A custom Metal shader compiled through PyTorch fused causal matrix products and online softmax. Half matrix operands with float32 scores, softmax totals, and output accumulation passed comfortably. A shared memory implementation with thirty two query rows and thirty two keys took 10.53 ms. Sixty four query rows took 10.89 ms; explicit loop unrolling took 10.72 ms. Thirty two queries and sixty four keys, with inputs converted to half beforehand, regressed to 15.29 ms.

Removing shared memory and barriers helped slightly: independent SIMD groups each process eight query rows, load matrices directly from half input buffers, and keep probabilities in registers. Thirty two key tiles and float32 accumulators took 10.38 ms.

Half precision score accumulation failed tolerance: the benchmark reported relative error 0.00202, and a separate check reported 0.00230. No valid time was obtained for that submission.

Keeping float32 scores but accumulating value products in half over thirty two keys took 9.78 ms; benchmark error reached 0.0019. Splitting those products into sixteen key blocks before adding them to float32 output accumulators improved both speed and accuracy: 9.44 ms, 6.97 times faster, benchmark error 0.0012. This is the final implementation. Ten additional random input checks passed, with maximum relative error about 0.0010. The shader preserves stable online softmax and skips future key tiles.

## Agent 4

The timed problem is contiguous float32 causal attention with batch eight, eight heads, sequence length 1024, and head size 64. Half precision inputs, scores, and probabilities stayed within the relative error tolerance of 0.002. Keep softmax reductions and matrix accumulation in float32 and return float32.

The best measured kernel took 8.30 milliseconds, versus a 63.65 millisecond reference, for a 7.67 times speedup. It converts inputs to half precision, uses a custom Metal matrix kernel for scores, applies a custom causal softmax, and uses PyTorch for the probability times value matrix product. Score tiles have 64 query rows and 64 key rows, with 256 threads. Skip whole tiles above the causal diagonal. Eight SIMD groups each handle eight query rows using Metal SIMD group matrix operations. Accumulate scores in float32, store half, then scale by one eighth in softmax.

The successful softmax uses one 256 thread group per row, four scores per thread, SIMD reductions followed by shared memory reductions, and half output. Its barriers must protect shared scratch reuse.

Measured alternatives: built in float32 causal attention took 16.64 milliseconds; half precision built in attention took 11.67; PyTorch half matrix products with the successful custom softmax took 9.26. A softmax with one SIMD group per row and 32 scores per thread worsened that to 10.52.

Fully fused online attention was slower: query/key tile sizes 32/32, 64/32, and 64/64 took 10.45, 10.40, and 11.25 milliseconds. Reusing shared memory and retaining probabilities in registers with 128/64 tiles took 10.31. One trial failed compilation because Metal max is not a constant expression for array sizes; a ternary expression works.

All timed successful variants passed correctness. GPU contention changes reference times, so compare absolute kernel times too.
"""Make kernel() faster. It must return the same values as reference() in reference.py."""
import torch


def kernel(q, k, v):
    T = q.size(-2)
    s = q @ k.transpose(-1, -2) / q.size(-1) ** 0.5
    s = s.masked_fill(torch.ones(T, T, dtype=torch.bool, device=q.device).triu(1), float("-inf"))
    e = (s - s.max(-1, keepdim=True).values).exp()
    return (e / e.sum(-1, keepdim=True)) @ v
"""The reference: causal attention in plain PyTorch, the inputs it is timed on, and the tolerance. Do not edit."""
import torch

TOL = 2e-3  # largest allowed |kernel - reference| / max |reference|


def reference(q, k, v):
    T = q.size(-2)
    s = q @ k.transpose(-1, -2) / q.size(-1) ** 0.5
    s = s.masked_fill(torch.ones(T, T, dtype=torch.bool, device=q.device).triu(1), float("-inf"))
    e = (s - s.max(-1, keepdim=True).values).exp()
    return (e / e.sum(-1, keepdim=True)) @ v


def inputs():
    """Timed inputs: batch 8, 8 heads, 1024 positions, head size 64, float32 on the GPU."""
    return tuple(torch.randn(8, 8, 1024, 64, device="mps") for _ in range(3))
"""Time kernel.py against the reference on the GPU and print the result. You have 10 runs.
  python bench.py         submit kernel.py and wait for its result (usually under a minute)
  python bench.py --wait  wait again for the last submitted run (if the first command was cut off)"""
import os, shutil, sys, time
from pathlib import Path

LIMIT = 10
here = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
queue, results = here / "queue", here / "results"
queue.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
results.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
sent = sorted(queue.glob("run*.py"), key=lambda p: int(p.stem[3:]))

if "--wait" in sys.argv:
    if not sent:
        sys.exit("no run submitted yet")
    n = len(sent)
else:
    if len(sent) >= LIMIT:
        sys.exit(f"all {LIMIT} runs are used")
    n = len(sent) + 1
    tmp = queue / f".run{n}.tmp"
    shutil.copy(here / "kernel.py", tmp)
    os.rename(tmp, queue / f"run{n}.py")
    print(f"run {n} of {LIMIT} submitted.", flush=True)

out = results / f"run{n}.txt"
while not out.exists():
    time.sleep(1)
print(out.read_text().strip())
"""Check that kernel() gives the reference's answer on one set of inputs. No timing. Use it as often as you like."""
import torch
from kernel import kernel
from reference import TOL, inputs, reference

with torch.no_grad():
    x = inputs()
    want, got = reference(*x), kernel(*x)
    err = (got.float() - want).abs().max().item() / want.abs().max().item()
print(f"{'OK' if got.shape == want.shape and err <= TOL else 'WRONG'}: relative error {err:.2e} (allowed {TOL:.0e})")

2.8.0
Compiles compute shader from source and allows one to invoke kernels
    defined there from the comfort of Python runtime
    Example::

        >>> # xdoctest: +REQUIRES(env:TORCH_DOCTEST_MPS)
        >>> lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(
        ... "kernel void full(device float* out, constant float& val, uint idx [[thread_position_in_grid]]) { out[idx] = val; }"
        ...  )
        >>> x = torch.zeros(16, device="mps")
        >>> lib.full(x, 3.14)

The notes favor a fused Metal kernel with half precision matrix inputs and float32 accumulation. I’ll start with that approach to avoid writing the full attention matrix to memory.
exec
/bin/zsh -lc "cat > kernel.py <<'PY'
\"\"\"Fused tiled causal attention for MPS.\"\"\"
import torch

_BQ = 64
_BK = 64
_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
#include <metal_stdlib>
#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
using namespace metal;
constant uint BQ = 64;
constant uint BK = 64;
kernel void attention(device const half* Q [[buffer(0)]],
                      device const half* K [[buffer(1)]],
                      device const half* V [[buffer(2)]],
                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
    threadgroup float scores[BQ * BK];
    threadgroup half probs[BQ * BK];
    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
    uint r = lane / 4;
    uint c = lane % 4;
    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
        simdgroup_float8x8 acc[8];
        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) acc[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
        for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
            simdgroup_load(a,Q+base+row0*64+d,64);
            for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
                simdgroup_load(b,K+base+(kb+j*8)*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc[j],a,b,acc[j]);
            }
        }
        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) simdgroup_store(acc[j],scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
        float mx = -INFINITY;
        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
            float s = kb+j<=row0+r ? scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=s;
            mx=max(mx,s);
        }
        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,2));
        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
            float p=exp(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]-nm);
            sum+=p;
            probs[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
        }
        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,2);
        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
        }
        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
        for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
            simdgroup_load(a,probs+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
            for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
                simdgroup_load(b,V+base+(kb+d)*64+j*8,64);
                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
            }
        }
        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
    }
    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
    }
}
''')

def kernel(q, k, v):
    if q.shape "'!= (8, 8, 1024, 64):
        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
    out = torch.empty_like(q)
    _lib.attention(q.half(), k.half(), v.half(), out,
                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
    return out

+import torch

+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''

+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const half* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const half* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const half* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup float scores[BQ * BK];
+    threadgroup half probs[BQ * BK];
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = lane / 4;
+    uint c = lane % 4;

+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        simdgroup_float8x8 acc[8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) acc[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+        for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+            simdgroup_load(a,Q+base+row0*64+d,64);
+            for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,K+base+(kb+j*8)*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc[j],a,b,acc[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) simdgroup_store(acc[j],scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s = kb+j<=row0+r ? scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=s;
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,2));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;

+            float p=exp(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            probs[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,2);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;

+            simdgroup_load(a,probs+sg*8*BK+d,BK);

+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,V+base+(kb+d)*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q.half(), k.half(), v.half(), out,

diff --git a/kernel.py b/kernel.py
index e69de29bb2d1d6434b8b29ae775ad8c2e48c5391..9b9110fc74df096c6cf773bc9c3d25989be66f4d
--- a/kernel.py
+++ b/kernel.py
@@ -0,0 +1,89 @@
+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const half* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const half* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const half* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup float scores[BQ * BK];
+    threadgroup half probs[BQ * BK];
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = lane / 4;
+    uint c = lane % 4;
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        simdgroup_float8x8 acc[8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) acc[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+        for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+            simdgroup_load(a,Q+base+row0*64+d,64);
+            for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,K+base+(kb+j*8)*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc[j],a,b,acc[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) simdgroup_store(acc[j],scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s = kb+j<=row0+r ? scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=s;
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,2));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            probs[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,2);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+            simdgroup_load(a,probs+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+            for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,V+base+(kb+d)*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q.half(), k.half(), v.half(), out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+        }
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                uint col=j*8+c*2+e;
+                float s=kb+col<=row0+r ? acc[j].thread_elements()[e]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
+                acc[j].thread_elements()[e]=s;
+                mx=max(mx,s);
+            }
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        simdgroup_half8x8 probs[BK/8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                float p=exp(acc[j].thread_elements()[e]-nm);
+                sum+=p;
+                probs[j].thread_elements()[e]=half(p);
+            }
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a=probs[d/8];
+            for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,V+base+(kb+d)*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q.half(), k.half(), v.half(), out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const half* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const half* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const half* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        simdgroup_float8x8 acc[8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) acc[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+        for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+            simdgroup_load(a,Q+base+row0*64+d,64);
+            for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,K+base+(kb+j*8)*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc[j],a,b,acc[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                uint col=j*8+c*2+e;
+                float s=kb+col<=row0+r ? acc[j].thread_elements()[e]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
+                acc[j].thread_elements()[e]=s;
+                mx=max(mx,s);
+            }
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        simdgroup_half8x8 probs[BK/8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                float p=exp(acc[j].thread_elements()[e]-nm);
+                sum+=p;
+                probs[j].thread_elements()[e]=half(p);
+            }
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a=probs[d/8];
+            for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,V+base+(kb+d)*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')

+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const half* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const half* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const half* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        simdgroup_float8x8 acc[8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) acc[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+        for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+            simdgroup_load(a,Q+base+row0*64+d,64);
+            for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,K+base+(kb+j*8)*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc[j],a,b,acc[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                uint col=j*8+c*2+e;
+                float s=kb+col<=row0+r ? acc[j].thread_elements()[e]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
+                acc[j].thread_elements()[e]=s;
+                mx=max(mx,s);
+            }
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        simdgroup_half8x8 probs[BK/8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                float p=exp(acc[j].thread_elements()[e]-nm);
+                sum+=p;
+                probs[j].thread_elements()[e]=half(p);
+            }
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a=probs[d/8];

+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;

+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')

+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        simdgroup_float8x8 acc[8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) acc[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+        for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+            simdgroup_load(a,Q+base+row0*64+d,64);
+            for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,K+base+(kb+j*8)*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc[j],a,b,acc[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                uint col=j*8+c*2+e;
+                float s=kb+col<=row0+r ? acc[j].thread_elements()[e]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
+                acc[j].thread_elements()[e]=s;
+                mx=max(mx,s);
+            }
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        simdgroup_half8x8 probs[BK/8];
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
+                float p=exp(acc[j].thread_elements()[e]-nm);
+                sum+=p;
+                probs[j].thread_elements()[e]=half(p);
+            }
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+            simdgroup_half8x8 a=probs[d/8];
+            for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,V+base+(kb+d)*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q.half(), k.half(), v.half(), out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
import torch

_BQ = 64
_BK = 64
_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
#include <metal_stdlib>
#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
using namespace metal;
constant uint BQ = 64;
constant uint BK = 64;
kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
        simdgroup_float8x8 acc[8];
        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) acc[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
        for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
            simdgroup_half8x8 a;
            simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
            for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc[j],a,b,acc[j]);
            }
        }
        float mx = -INFINITY;
        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
                uint col=j*8+c*2+e;
                float s=kb+col<=row0+r ? acc[j].thread_elements()[e]*0.125f : -INFINITY;
                acc[j].thread_elements()[e]=s;
                mx=max(mx,s);
            }
        }
        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
        simdgroup_half8x8 probs[BK/8];
        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
            for(uint e=0;e<2;e++) {
                float p=exp(acc[j].thread_elements()[e]-nm);
                sum+=p;
                probs[j].thread_elements()[e]=half(p);
            }
        }
        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
        }
        for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
            simdgroup_half8x8 a=probs[d/8];
            for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
            }
        }
        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
    }
    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
    }
}
''')

def kernel(q, k, v):
    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
    out = torch.empty_like(q)
    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
    return out

+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')

+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,

+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];

+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);

+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;

+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')

+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

diff --git a/kernel.py b/kernel.py
index e69de29bb2d1d6434b8b29ae775ad8c2e48c5391..bf53682b6abb37ed7f498cdf03d7a81b1bfcab31
--- a/kernel.py
+++ b/kernel.py
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],

+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
import torch

_BQ = 64
_BK = 64
_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
#include <metal_stdlib>
#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
using namespace metal;
constant uint BQ = 64;
constant uint BK = 64;
kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
    #pragma unroll
    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
            }
            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
        }
        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
        float mx = -INFINITY;
        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
            mx=max(mx,s);
        }
        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
            sum+=p;
            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
        }
        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
        #pragma unroll
        #pragma unroll
    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
        }
        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
        #pragma unroll
        #pragma unroll
    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
            }
        }
        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
    }
    #pragma unroll
    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
    }
}
''')

def kernel(q, k, v):
    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
    out = torch.empty_like(q)
    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
    return out

+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+++ b/kernel.py
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

diff --git a/kernel.py b/kernel.py
index e69de29bb2d1d6434b8b29ae775ad8c2e48c5391..bf53682b6abb37ed7f498cdf03d7a81b1bfcab31
--- a/kernel.py
+++ b/kernel.py
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out

+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')

+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))

diff --git a/kernel.py b/kernel.py
index e69de29bb2d1d6434b8b29ae775ad8c2e48c5391..bf53682b6abb37ed7f498cdf03d7a81b1bfcab31
--- a/kernel.py
+++ b/kernel.py
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+"""Fused tiled causal attention for MPS."""
+import torch
+
+_BQ = 64
+_BK = 64
+_lib = torch.mps.compile_shader(r'''
+#include <metal_stdlib>
+#include <metal_simdgroup_matrix>
+using namespace metal;
+constant uint BQ = 64;
+constant uint BK = 64;
+kernel void attention(device const float* Q [[buffer(0)]],
+                      device const float* K [[buffer(1)]],
+                      device const float* V [[buffer(2)]],
+                      device float* O [[buffer(3)]],
+                      uint3 group [[threadgroup_position_in_grid]],
+                      uint lane [[thread_index_in_simdgroup]],
+                      uint sg [[simdgroup_index_in_threadgroup]]) {
+    threadgroup half qs[BQ*64];
+    threadgroup half ks[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half vs[BK*64];
+    threadgroup half scores[BQ*BK];
+    uint tid=sg*32+lane;
+    uint row0 = group.x * BQ + sg * 8;
+    uint base = group.y * 1024 * 64;
+    uint r = (lane % 8) / 2 + (lane / 16) * 4;
+    uint c = lane % 2 + ((lane / 8) % 2) * 2;
+    for(uint i=tid;i<BQ*64;i+=256) qs[i]=half(Q[base+group.x*BQ*64+i]);
+    threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    float m = -INFINITY, l = 0.0f;
+    simdgroup_float8x8 out[8];
+    for (uint j=0;j<8;j++) out[j] = simdgroup_float8x8(0.0f);
+    for (uint kb=0;kb<group.x*BQ+BQ;kb+=BK) {
+        for(uint i=tid;i<BK*64;i+=256) {ks[i]=half(K[base+kb*64+i]);vs[i]=half(V[base+kb*64+i]);}
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<BK/8;j++) {
+            simdgroup_float8x8 acc(0.0f);
+            for(uint d=0;d<64;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a,b;
+                simdgroup_load(a,qs+sg*8*64+d,64);
+                simdgroup_load(b,ks+j*8*64+d,64,ulong2(0),true);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(acc,a,b,acc);
+            }
+            simdgroup_half8x8 h;
+            h.thread_elements()[0]=half(acc.thread_elements()[0]*0.125f);
+            h.thread_elements()[1]=half(acc.thread_elements()[1]*0.125f);
+            simdgroup_store(h,scores+sg*8*BK+j*8,BK);
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        float mx = -INFINITY;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float s=kb+j<=row0+r ? float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]) : -INFINITY;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(s);
+            mx=max(mx,s);
+        }
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,1));
+        mx=max(mx,simd_shuffle_xor(mx,8));
+        float nm=max(m,mx), alpha=exp(m-nm), sum=0;
+        for(uint j=c;j<BK;j+=4) {
+            float p=exp(float(scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j])-nm);
+            sum+=p;
+            scores[(sg*8+r)*BK+j]=half(p);
+        }
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,1);
+        sum+=simd_shuffle_xor(sum,8);
+        l=l*alpha+sum; m=nm;
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            out[j].thread_elements()[0]*=alpha;
+            out[j].thread_elements()[1]*=alpha;
+        }
+        simdgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+        for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+            for(uint d=0;d<BK;d+=8) {
+                simdgroup_half8x8 a;
+                simdgroup_load(a,scores+sg*8*BK+d,BK);
+                simdgroup_half8x8 b;
+                simdgroup_load(b,vs+d*64+j*8,64);
+                simdgroup_multiply_accumulate(out[j],a,b,out[j]);
+            }
+        }
+        threadgroup_barrier(mem_flags::mem_threadgroup);
+    }
+    for(uint j=0;j<8;j++) {
+        out[j].thread_elements()[0]/=l;
+        out[j].thread_elements()[1]/=l;
+        simdgroup_store(out[j],O+base+row0*64+j*8,64);
+    }
+}
+''')
+
+def kernel(q, k, v):
+    if q.shape != (8, 8, 1024, 64):
+        return torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
+    out = torch.empty_like(q)
+    _lib.attention(q, k, v, out,
+                   threads=(16*256,64,1), group_size=(256,1,1))
+    return out
